Simulations on a planar 2-link manipulator and a magnetically actuated needle demonstrate smooth trajectory tracking while avoiding singular configurations and reducing control input spikes by up to 100x compared to the nominal controller.

</details>


### [111] [Human-in-the-Loop Pareto Optimization: Trade-off Characterization for Assist-as-Needed Training and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23777)
*Harun Tolasa,Volkan Patoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于人机闭环的帕累托优化方法，用于量化运动学习任务中任务难度与用户表现之间的权衡关系，并展示了该方法在辅助训练协议设计、个体训练进展评估和用户间公平比较三个应用场景中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动技能训练和物理康复中，任务难度与用户表现之间存在固有的权衡关系。准确描述这种权衡关系对于评估用户表现、设计按需辅助协议以及评估训练协议效果至关重要。传统方法难以系统量化这种复杂关系，特别是在用户无法独立完成任务的情况下。

Method: 提出人机闭环帕累托优化方法，采用贝叶斯多目标优化技术系统高效地进行权衡关系表征。该方法使用混合模型：任务表现通过定量指标测量，感知挑战水平通过定性指标捕获。通过用户研究验证了该方法的可行性，并在具有触觉反馈的手动技能训练任务中展示了三个应用场景。

Result: 成功实现了任务难度与用户表现之间权衡关系的系统表征。该方法能够：1）设计按需辅助训练协议并评估其相对于基线自适应辅助协议的群体水平效果；2）在训练前后进行个体水平权衡比较，实现不同辅助水平下训练进展的公平评估；3）通过捕获所有可行辅助水平下每个用户的最佳可能表现，实现不同用户间的公平性能比较。

Conclusion: 提出的人机闭环帕累托优化方法能够有效表征运动学习任务中任务难度与用户表现之间的权衡关系。该方法为按需辅助协议设计、个体训练进展评估和用户间公平比较提供了系统化框架，特别是在用户无法独立完成任务的情况下仍能提供有价值的评估见解，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: During human motor skill training and physical rehabilitation, there is an inherent trade-off between task difficulty and user performance. Characterizing this trade-off is crucial for evaluating user performance, designing assist-as-needed (AAN) protocols, and assessing the efficacy of training protocols. In this study, we propose a novel human-in-the-loop (HiL) Pareto optimization approach to characterize the trade-off between task performance and the perceived challenge level of motor learning or rehabilitation tasks. We adapt Bayesian multi-criteria optimization to systematically and efficiently perform HiL Pareto characterizations. Our HiL optimization employs a hybrid model that measures performance with a quantitative metric, while the perceived challenge level is captured with a qualitative metric. We demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed HiL Pareto characterization through a user study. Furthermore, we present the utility of the framework through three use cases in the context of a manual skill training task with haptic feedback. First, we demonstrate how the characterized trade-off can be used to design a sample AAN training protocol for a motor learning task and to evaluate the group-level efficacy of the proposed AAN protocol relative to a baseline adaptive assistance protocol. Second, we demonstrate that individual-level comparisons of the trade-offs characterized before and after the training session enable fair evaluation of training progress under different assistance levels. This evaluation method is more general than standard performance evaluations, as it can provide insights even when users cannot perform the task without assistance. Third, we show that the characterized trade-offs also enable fair performance comparisons among different users, as they capture the best possible performance of each user under all feasible assistance levels.

</details>


### [112] [Object Search in Partially-Known Environments via LLM-informed Model-based Planning and Prompt Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23800)
*Abhishek Paudel,Abhish Khanal,Raihan I. Arnob,Shahriar Hossain,Gregory J. Stein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的模型规划框架和提示选择方法，用于部分已知环境中的物体搜索任务，通过LLM估计位置搜索概率并结合环境地图的移动成本来指导规划，同时利用离线重放方法实现部署时的快速提示和LLM选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分已知环境中进行物体搜索时，需要有效结合LLM的语义理解能力和传统规划方法，同时解决如何快速选择最佳提示和LLM模型的问题，以提高搜索效率和降低决策成本。

Method: 1. LLM-informed模型规划框架：使用LLM估计在不同位置找到目标物体的概率统计，结合从环境地图提取的移动成本，实例化规划模型；2. 基于离线重放方法的提示选择：利用部署时模型选择技术，通过类似bandit的方法快速选择最佳提示和LLM。

Result: 仿真实验显示：1. LLM-informed规划方法比完全依赖LLM的基线策略提升11.8%，比乐观策略提升39.2%；2. Bandit-like选择方法比基线UCB bandit选择降低6.5%的平均成本和33.8%的累计遗憾；真实机器人实验在公寓环境中验证了类似改进。

Conclusion: 提出的LLM-informed模型规划框架有效结合了LLM的语义理解和传统规划优势，同时创新的提示选择方法能够在部署时快速优化性能，为部分已知环境中的物体搜索任务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We present a novel LLM-informed model-based planning framework, and a novel prompt selection method, for object search in partially-known environments. Our approach uses an LLM to estimate statistics about the likelihood of finding the target object when searching various locations throughout the scene that, combined with travel costs extracted from the environment map, are used to instantiate a model, thus using the LLM to inform planning and achieve effective search performance. Moreover, the abstraction upon which our approach relies is amenable to deployment-time model selection via the recent offline replay approach, an insight we leverage to enable fast prompt and LLM selection during deployment. Simulation experiments demonstrate that our LLM-informed model-based planning approach outperforms the baseline planning strategy that fully relies on LLM and optimistic strategy with as much as 11.8% and 39.2% improvements respectively, and our bandit-like selection approach enables quick selection of best prompts and LLMs resulting in 6.5% lower average cost and 33.8% lower average cumulative regret over baseline UCB bandit selection. Real-robot experiments in an apartment demonstrate similar improvements and so further validate our approach.

</details>


### [113] [AgentChemist: A Multi-Agent Experimental Robotic Platform Integrating Chemical Perception and Precise Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23886)
*Xiangyi Wei,Fei Wang,Haotian Zhang,Xin An,Haitian Zhu,Lianrui Hu,Yang Li,Changbo Wang,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多智能体机器人平台通过任务分解、动态调度和自适应控制解决化学实验室自动化的长尾分布问题，实现灵活的实验执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统化学实验室自动化受限于刚性工作流程，难以适应实验任务的长尾分布（即多样、低频、演化的操作），无法处理新颖反应条件、非常规仪器配置和意外程序变化。

Method: 提出多智能体机器人平台，采用协作任务分解、动态调度和自适应控制；集成化学感知进行实时反应监测，通过反馈驱动执行，基于实验状态动态调整而非固定脚本。

Result: 通过酸碱滴定验证，平台展示了自主进度跟踪、自适应分配控制和可靠的端到端实验执行能力。

Conclusion: 该平台通过提高对多样化实验室场景的泛化能力，为智能、灵活、可扩展的实验室自动化提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Chemical laboratory automation has long been constrained by rigid workflows and poor adaptability to the long-tail distribution of experimental tasks. While most automated platforms perform well on a narrow set of standardized procedures, real laboratories involve diverse, infrequent, and evolving operations that fall outside predefined protocols. This mismatch prevents existing systems from generalizing to novel reaction conditions, uncommon instrument configurations, and unexpected procedural variations. We present a multi-agent robotic platform designed to address this long-tail challenge through collaborative task decomposition, dynamic scheduling, and adaptive control. The system integrates chemical perception for real-time reaction monitoring with feedback-driven execution, enabling it to adjust actions based on evolving experimental states rather than fixed scripts. Validation via acid-base titration demonstrates autonomous progress tracking, adaptive dispensing control, and reliable end-to-end experiment execution. By improving generalization across diverse laboratory scenarios, this platform provides a practical pathway toward intelligent, flexible, and scalable laboratory automation.

</details>


### [114] [Event-Driven Proactive Assistive Manipulation with Grounded Vision-Language Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23950)
*Fengkai Liu,Hao Su,Haozhuang Chi,Rui Geng,Congzhi Ren,Xuqing Liu,Yucheng Xu,Yuichi Ohsita,Liyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从请求驱动转向事件驱动的主动协作框架，通过监控工作空间状态变化而非用户指令来触发机器人辅助行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类团队协作中，伙伴通常通过观察动作结果推断下一步帮助，而非等待指令。现有机器人协作多基于用户指令驱动，缺乏这种自然的主动性

Method: 提出事件驱动框架：1) 事件监控器跟踪交互进度，在事件完成时提取稳定的前后状态快照；2) 规划器分析状态转换推断任务目标，决定是否干预；3) 使用动作基元和整数ID引用对象生成可执行、可验证的辅助动作序列

Result: 在真实桌面数字块协作任务中评估，证明显式的前后状态变化证据能提高可解场景的主动完成率和不可解场景的适当等待

Conclusion: 事件驱动的主动辅助框架通过监控状态转换而非依赖用户指令，实现了更自然流畅的人机协作，提高了协作效率和适应性

Abstract: Assistance in collaborative manipulation is often initiated by user instructions, making high-level reasoning request-driven. In fluent human teamwork, however, partners often infer the next helpful step from the observed outcome of an action rather than waiting for instructions. Motivated by this, we introduce a shift from request-driven assistance to event-driven proactive assistance, where robot actions are initiated by workspace state transitions induced by human--object interactions rather than user-provided task instructions. To this end, we propose an event-driven framework that tracks interaction progress with an event monitor and, upon event completion, extracts stabilized pre/post snapshots that characterize the resulting state transition. Given the stabilized snapshots, the planner analyzes the implied state transition to infer a task-level goal and decide whether to intervene; if so, it generates a sequence of assistive actions. To make outputs executable and verifiable, we restrict actions to a set of action primitives and reference objects via integer IDs. We evaluate the framework on a real tabletop number-block collaboration task, demonstrating that explicit pre/post state-change evidence improves proactive completion on solvable scenes and appropriate waiting on unsolvable ones.

</details>


### [115] [MonoSIM: An open source SIL framework for Ackermann Vehicular Systems with Monocular Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23965)
*Shantanu Rahman,Nayeb Hasin,Mainul Islam,Md. Zubair Alom Rony,Golam Sarowar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源SIL仿真平台，用于阿克曼转向自动驾驶车辆的研究与教育，支持低成本AGV开发和多智能体系统研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自动驾驶阿克曼车辆研究提供简单易用的仿真平台，特别适合小型实验平台（如XTENTH-CAR）和低成本AGV开发，解决算法测试验证环境不足的问题

Method: 基于开源工具构建SIL仿真框架，采用单目摄像头视觉系统，通过滑动窗口车道线检测方法降低计算开销，支持MPC和PID等控制算法的灵活测试与验证

Result: 平台成功验证了MPC和PID控制算法的有效性，为算法验证提供了可靠环境，适合未来多智能体系统研究、教育应用和低成本AGV开发

Conclusion: 该开源SIL仿真平台为自动驾驶阿克曼车辆研究提供了简单、灵活且可靠的算法测试环境，是未来多智能体系统研究、教育和低成本AGV开发的理想工具

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source Software-in-the-Loop (SIL) simulation platform designed for autonomous Ackerman vehicle research and education. The proposed framework focuses on simplicity, while making it easy to work with small-scale experimental setups, such as the XTENTH-CAR platform. The system was designed using open source tools, creating an environment with a monocular camera vision system to capture stimuli from it with minimal computational overhead through a sliding window based lane detection method. The platform supports a flexible algorithm testing and validation environment, allowing researchers to implement and compare various control strategies within an easy-to-use virtual environment. To validate the working of the platform, Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) algorithms were implemented within the SIL framework. The results confirm that the platform provides a reliable environment for algorithm verification, making it an ideal tool for future multi-agent system research, educational purposes, and low-cost AGV development. Our code is available at https://github.com/shantanu404/monosim.git.

</details>


### [116] [Robust Distributed Cooperative Path-Following and Local Replanning for Multi-UAVs Under Differentiated Low-Altitude Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23968)
*Zimao Sheng,Zirui Yu,Hong'an Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多固定翼无人机在复杂DEM低空空域中的协同路径跟踪控制策略，通过分布式时间协调、鲁棒制导律和实时避障重规划解决风场扰动、突发障碍和时同步需求等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多无人机协同路径跟踪方面存在三个主要不足：缺乏高效的分布式协调机制来实现3D差异化路径的时间一致性跟踪；未能量化对扰动的鲁棒性；缺乏有效的在线避障重规划能力。这些不足限制了多无人机在复杂低空环境中的协同作业能力。

Method: 1) 通过时间指标量化分布式协同路径跟踪问题，采用分布式通信协议确保一致性；2) 开发纵向-横向前视角调整方法结合鲁棒制导律，在风扰动下实现路径跟踪误差的有限时间稳定；3) 设计具有最小时间成本的高效局部路径重规划方法，用于实时在线避障。

Result: 实验验证表明，所提出的策略在复杂DEM低空空域中有效解决了多无人机协同路径跟踪问题，实现了时间一致性跟踪、鲁棒抗扰和实时避障，表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的协同控制策略成功解决了多固定翼无人机在复杂低空环境中的路径跟踪挑战，通过分布式协调、鲁棒制导和实时重规划的综合方法，为多无人机协同作业提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multiple fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicles (multi-UAVs) encounter significant challenges in cooperative path following over complex Digital Elevation Model (DEM) low-altitude airspace, including wind field disturbances, sudden obstacles, and requirements of distributed temporal synchronization during differentiated path tracking. Existing methods lack efficient distributed coordination mechanisms for time-consistent tracking of 3D differentiated paths, fail to quantify robustness against disturbances, and lack effective online obstacle avoidance replanning capabilities. To address these gaps, a cooperative control strategy is proposed: first, the distributed cooperative path-following problem is quantified via time indices, and consistency is ensured through a distributed communication protocol; second, a longitudinal-lateral look-ahead angle adjustment method coupled with a robust guidance law is developed to achieve finite-time stabilization of path following error to zero under wind disturbances; third, an efficient local path replanning method with minimal time cost is designed for real-time online obstacle avoidance.Experimental validations demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the $\ $proposed strategy.

</details>


### [117] [SafeFlow: Real-Time Text-Driven Humanoid Whole-Body Control via Physics-Guided Rectified Flow and Selective Safety Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23983)
*Hanbyel Cho,Sang-Hun Kim,Jeonguk Kang,Donghan Koo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeFlow：结合物理引导运动生成与3阶段安全门控的文本驱动人形机器人全身控制框架，解决运动生成中的物理幻觉问题，提升真实机器人执行的安全性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动学的文本驱动运动生成方法存在物理幻觉问题，生成的运动轨迹在物理上不可行，难以被下游运动跟踪控制器跟踪，且在实际部署中存在安全隐患。这些问题源于缺乏明确的物理感知目标，在分布外用户输入下更为严重。

Method: 采用两级架构：高层使用物理引导的修正流匹配在VAE潜在空间中生成运动轨迹，并通过Reflow加速采样；3阶段安全门控包括：1) 基于马氏距离检测语义分布外提示，2) 通过方向敏感性差异度量过滤不稳定生成，3) 执行关节和速度限制等硬运动学约束。

Result: 在Unitree G1上的广泛实验表明，SafeFlow在成功率、物理合规性和推理速度方面优于先前的基于扩散的方法，同时保持了多样化的表达能力。

Conclusion: SafeFlow通过结合物理引导的运动生成和显式风险指标驱动的安全门控，有效解决了文本驱动运动生成中的物理幻觉问题，实现了更安全、更可靠的实时人形机器人控制。

Abstract: Recent advances in real-time interactive text-driven motion generation have enabled humanoids to perform diverse behaviors. However, kinematics-only generators often exhibit physical hallucinations, producing motion trajectories that are physically infeasible to track with a downstream motion tracking controller or unsafe for real-world deployment. These failures often arise from the lack of explicit physics-aware objectives for real-robot execution and become more severe under out-of-distribution (OOD) user inputs. Hence, we propose SafeFlow, a text-driven humanoid whole-body control framework that combines physics-guided motion generation with a 3-Stage Safety Gate driven by explicit risk indicators. SafeFlow adopts a two-level architecture. At the high level, we generate motion trajectories using Physics-Guided Rectified Flow Matching in a VAE latent space to improve real-robot executability, and further accelerate sampling via Reflow to reduce the number of function evaluations (NFE) for real-time control. The 3-Stage Safety Gate enables selective execution by detecting semantic OOD prompts using a Mahalanobis score in text-embedding space, filtering unstable generations via a directional sensitivity discrepancy metric, and enforcing final hard kinematic constraints such as joint and velocity limits before passing the generated trajectory to a low-level motion tracking controller. Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate that SafeFlow outperforms prior diffusion-based methods in success rate, physical compliance, and inference speed, while maintaining diverse expressiveness.

</details>


### [118] [MIRROR: Visual Motion Imitation via Real-time Retargeting and Teleoperation with Parallel Differential Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23995)
*Junheng Li,Lizhi Yang,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU并行化、基于延拓的微分逆运动学方法，用于实时人形机器人遥操作，解决约束诱导的局部极小值问题，提高安全性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时人形机器人遥操作需要既响应迅速又能在运动学冗余和自碰撞约束下保持安全的逆运动学求解器。传统微分逆运动学方法由于局部线性化更新，容易在关节极限、奇异点或碰撞边界附近陷入局部极小值，导致不安全或停滞行为。

Method: 1. GPU并行化的基于延拓的微分逆运动学方法；2. 并行评估多个约束逆运动学二次规划问题；3. 结合自碰撞避免控制屏障函数；4. 基于李雅普诺夫的进展准则选择能减少最终全局任务空间误差的更新；5. 与视觉骨骼姿态估计管道配对，实现实时上身遥操作。

Result: 该方法在THEMIS人形机器人硬件上实现了鲁棒的实时上身遥操作，在实际任务中表现出色，能够从约束诱导的局部极小值中有效逃脱，同时保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 提出的GPU并行化、基于延拓的微分逆运动学方法显著改善了实时人形机器人遥操作中的约束安全性和稳定性，有效解决了传统方法在关节极限、奇异点和碰撞边界附近陷入局部极小值的问题。

Abstract: Real-time humanoid teleoperation requires inverse kinematics (IK) solvers that are both responsive and constraint-safe under kinematic redundancy and self-collision constraints. While differential IK enables efficient online retargeting, its locally linearized updates are inherently basin-dependent and often become trapped near joint limits, singularities, or active collision boundaries, leading to unsafe or stagnant behavior. We propose a GPU-parallelized, continuation-based differential IK that improves escape from such constraint-induced local minima while preserving real-time performance, promoting safety and stability. Multiple constrained IK quadratic programs are evaluated in parallel, together with a self-collision avoidance control barrier function (CBF), and a Lyapunov-based progression criterion selects updates that reduce the final global task-space error. The method is paired with a visual skeletal pose estimation pipeline that enables robust, real-time upper-body teleoperation on the THEMIS humanoid robot hardware in real-world tasks.

</details>


### [119] [QuadFM: Foundational Text-Driven Quadruped Motion Dataset for Generation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24021)
*Li Gao,Fuzhi Yang,Jianhui Chen,Liu Liu,Yao Zheng,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadFM是首个大规模、高保真的四足机器人基础运动数据集，包含11,784个运动片段和35,352个语言描述，支持文本到运动生成和通用运动控制。论文还提出了Gen2Control RL统一框架，在边缘硬件上实现实时运动合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四足机器人运动数据集局限于少数运动基元（如行走、小跑、坐下），缺乏多样化的行为和丰富的语言语义基础，这限制了敏捷、直观的人机交互能力。需要填补基础运动资源中整合多样化运动、情感表达行为和丰富语言语义的空白。

Method: 1) 构建QuadFM数据集：包含11,784个精选运动片段，涵盖运动、交互和情感表达行为，每个片段有三层标注（细粒度动作标签、交互场景、自然语言命令）；2) 提出Gen2Control RL统一框架：联合训练通用运动控制器和文本到运动生成器，支持端到端推理；3) 在配备NVIDIA Orin的真实四足机器人上实现实时运动合成（延迟<500毫秒）。

Result: 1) 创建了首个大规模四足机器人基础运动数据集QuadFM；2) 提出的系统在仿真和真实世界中都能生成逼真、多样化的运动，同时保持稳健的物理交互；3) 在边缘硬件上实现了实时运动合成（延迟<500毫秒）；4) 数据集将在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: QuadFM填补了四足机器人基础运动资源的空白，通过整合多样化运动、情感表达和语言语义，为敏捷、直观的人机交互提供了重要支持。提出的Gen2Control RL框架实现了高效的端到端运动生成和控制，在边缘硬件上达到实时性能，推动了四足机器人运动生成和控制技术的发展。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in quadrupedal robotics, a critical gap persists in foundational motion resources that holistically integrate diverse locomotion, emotionally expressive behaviors, and rich language semantics-essential for agile, intuitive human-robot interaction. Current quadruped motion datasets are limited to a few mocap primitives (e.g., walk, trot, sit) and lack diverse behaviors with rich language grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce Quadruped Foundational Motion (QuadFM) , the first large-scale, ultra-high-fidelity dataset designed for text-to-motion generation and general motion control. QuadFM contains 11,784 curated motion clips spanning locomotion, interactive, and emotion-expressive behaviors (e.g., dancing, stretching, peeing), each with three-layer annotation-fine-grained action labels, interaction scenarios, and natural language commands-totaling 35,352 descriptions to support language-conditioned understanding and command execution.
  We further propose Gen2Control RL, a unified framework that jointly trains a general motion controller and a text-to-motion generator, enabling efficient end-to-end inference on edge hardware. On a real quadruped robot with an NVIDIA Orin, our system achieves real-time motion synthesis (<500 ms latency). Simulation and real-world results show realistic, diverse motions while maintaining robust physical interaction. The dataset will be released at https://github.com/GaoLii/QuadFM.

</details>


### [120] [PCHC: Enabling Preference Conditioned Humanoid Control via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24047)
*Huanyu Li,Dewei Wang,Xinmiao Wang,Xinzhe Liu,Peng Liu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于多目标强化学习的偏好条件人形机器人控制框架，通过单一策略实现多样行为，无需训练多个策略逼近帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习方法在人形机器人控制中采用固定权重策略，只能产生单一次优策略，无法提供多样化的多目标控制解决方案。需要一种能够根据偏好条件实时调整目标优先级的方法。

Method: 提出偏好条件人形控制框架，利用多目标强化学习。引入基于Beta分布的对齐机制，通过偏好向量调制混合专家模块，使单一策略能展现广泛行为谱系。

Result: 在两个代表性人形机器人任务上进行验证，大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，该框架能让机器人根据输入偏好条件实时自适应调整目标优先级。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标强化学习框架成功实现了偏好条件人形控制，通过单一策略提供了多样化的行为解决方案，克服了传统方法需要训练多个策略的局限性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often need to balance competing objectives, such as maximizing speed while minimizing energy consumption. While current reinforcement learning (RL) methods can master complex skills like fall recovery and perceptive locomotion, they are constrained by fixed weighting strategies that produce a single suboptimal policy, rather than providing a diverse set of solutions for sophisticated multi-objective control. In this paper, we propose a novel framework leveraging Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) to achieve Preference-Conditioned Humanoid Control (PCHC). Unlike conventional methods that require training a series of policies to approximate the Pareto front, our framework enables a single, preference-conditioned policy to exhibit a wide spectrum of diverse behaviors. To effectively integrate these requirements, we introduce a Beta distribution-based alignment mechanism based on preference vectors modulating a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) module. We validated our approach on two representative humanoid tasks. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework allows the robot to adaptively shift its objective priorities in real-time based on the input preference condition.

</details>


### [121] [SOMA: Strategic Orchestration and Memory-Augmented System for Vision-Language-Action Model Robustness via In-Context Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24060)
*Zhuoran Li,Zhiyang Li,Kaijun Zhou,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOMA是一个战略编排和记忆增强系统，通过在线双记忆检索增强生成、归因驱动的LLM编排器和可扩展模型上下文协议干预，以及离线记忆整合模块，显著提升了冻结VLA策略在OOD任务中的鲁棒性，平均绝对成功率提升56.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型作为通用机器人控制器，在分布外任务中由于缺乏长期记忆、因果故障归因和动态干预能力，对感知噪声和环境变化的鲁棒性存在根本性限制。

Method: SOMA通过在线对比双记忆检索增强生成(RAG)、归因驱动的大语言模型(LLM)编排器和可扩展模型上下文协议(MCP)干预，以及离线记忆整合模块，在不进行参数微调的情况下升级冻结VLA策略。

Result: 在三个骨干模型(pi0、pi0.5和SmolVLA)上，在LIBERO-PRO和LIBERO-SOMA基准测试中，SOMA实现了平均56.6%的绝对成功率提升，其中长时域任务链的绝对改进达到89.1%。

Conclusion: SOMA通过战略编排和记忆增强机制，显著提升了VLA模型在分布外任务中的鲁棒性和适应性，为机器人控制提供了有效的上下文适应解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the promise of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models as generalist robotic controllers, their robustness against perceptual noise and environmental variations in out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks remains fundamentally limited by the absence of long-term memory, causal failure attribution, and dynamic intervention capability. To address this, we propose SOMA, a Strategic Orchestration and Memory-Augmented System that upgrades frozen VLA policies for robust in-context adaptation without parameter fine-tuning. Specifically, SOMA operates through an online pipeline of contrastive Dual-Memory Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), an Attribution-Driven Large-Language-Model (LLM) Orchestrator, and extensible Model Context Protocol (MCP) interventions, while an offline Memory Consolidation module continuously distills the execution traces into reliable priors. Experimental evaluations across three backbone models (pi0, pi0.5, and SmolVLA) on LIBERO-PRO and our proposed LIBERO-SOMA benchmarks demonstrate that SOMA achieves an average absolute success rate gain of 56.6%. This includes a significant absolute improvement of 89.1% in long-horizon task chaining. Project page and source code are available at: https://github.com/LZY-1021/SOMA.

</details>


### [122] [Equivariant Filter Transformations for Consistent and Efficient Visual--Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24130)
*Chungeng Tian,Fenghua He,Ning Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种等变滤波器变换方法，通过建立不同对称性EqF间的解析联系，实现系统化一致性设计和高效实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉-惯性导航中，等变滤波器的一致性设计缺乏系统性方法，且实现时计算负担较重，需要更有效的设计原则和实现策略

Method: 1) 形式化全局系统状态到局部误差状态的映射，证明其诱导任意两个EqF误差状态间的非奇异线性变换；2) 推导相关线性化误差状态系统和非可观测子空间的变换规律；3) 提出两种高效实现策略，利用具有块对角结构的简单EqF雅可比矩阵加速协方差运算

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真和真实世界实验验证了所提方法在精度和运行时间方面的有效性，实现了既保持一致性又降低计算负担的目标

Conclusion: 该EqF变换方法为视觉-惯性导航提供了一种系统化的一致性设计框架，通过变换局部坐标图合成具有状态无关非可观测子空间的EqF，避免了特设的对称性分析，同时通过高效实现策略解决了计算效率问题

Abstract: This paper presents an equivariant filter (EqF) transformation approach for visual--inertial navigation. By establishing analytical links between EqFs with different symmetries, the proposed approach enables systematic consistency design and efficient implementation. First, we formalize the mapping from the global system state to the local error-state and prove that it induces a nonsingular linear transformation between the error-states of any two EqFs. Second, we derive transformation laws for the associated linearized error-state systems and unobservable subspaces. These results yield a general consistency design principle: for any unobservable system, a consistent EqF with a state-independent unobservable subspace can be synthesized by transforming the local coordinate chart, thereby avoiding ad hoc symmetry analysis. Third, to mitigate the computational burden arising from the non-block-diagonal Jacobians required for consistency, we propose two efficient implementation strategies. These strategies exploit the Jacobians of a simpler EqF with block-diagonal structure to accelerate covariance operations while preserving consistency. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations and real-world experiments validate the proposed approach in terms of both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [123] [Accelerated Spline-Based Time-Optimal Motion Planning with Continuous Safety Guarantees for Non-Differentially Flat Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24133)
*Dries Dirckx,Jan Swevers,Wilm Decré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦分离超平面确定与最优控制问题的方法，将连续碰撞避免的非凸约束转化为线性约束，显著降低计算时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于样条的运动规划方法将分离超平面参数作为决策变量包含在最优控制问题中，导致计算成本高昂，需要在安全保证和计算复杂度之间进行权衡

Method: 将分离定理作为独立的分类问题处理，通过线性系统或二次规划求解分离超平面，从优化变量中消除超平面参数，将非凸约束转化为线性约束

Result: 在障碍物丰富的环境中，与完全耦合方法相比，轨迹计算时间减少近60%，同时保持严格的连续安全保证

Conclusion: 解耦方法有效解决了连续碰撞避免的计算瓶颈，在保持安全性的同时显著提高了运动规划效率

Abstract: Generating time-optimal, collision-free trajectories for autonomous mobile robots involves a fundamental trade-off between guaranteeing safety and managing computational complexity. State-of-the-art approaches formulate spline-based motion planning as a single Optimal Control Problem (OCP) but often suffer from high computational cost because they include separating hyperplane parameters as decision variables to enforce continuous collision avoidance. This paper presents a novel method that alleviates this bottleneck by decoupling the determination of separating hyperplanes from the OCP. By treating the separation theorem as an independent classification problem solvable via a linear system or quadratic program, the proposed method eliminates hyperplane parameters from the optimisation variables, effectively transforming non-convex constraints into linear ones. Experimental validation demonstrates that this decoupled approach reduces trajectory computation times up to almost 60% compared to fully coupled methods in obstacle-rich environments, while maintaining rigorous continuous safety guarantees.

</details>


### [124] [Goal-Oriented Reactive Simulation for Closed-Loop Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24155)
*Harsh Yadav,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于高频、滚动时域轨迹预测的在线闭环训练范式，通过目标导向的Transformer场景解码器实现反应一致性，显著提升碰撞避免能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前轨迹预测模型主要在开环方式下训练，在真实世界闭环部署时会导致协变量偏移和误差累积；依赖静态数据集或非反应式日志回放模拟器切断了交互循环，使自车无法学习主动协商周围交通

Method: 提出在线闭环训练范式，引入目标导向的Transformer基场景解码器，创建固有反应式训练模拟；通过混合开环数据和模拟自诱导状态，使模型学习纠正自身执行误差的恢复行为；采用混合模拟结合反应式与非反应式周围智能体

Result: 闭环训练显著增强高频重规划下的碰撞避免能力：nuScenes上相对碰撞率降低27.0%，密集DeepScenario交叉口降低79.5%；混合模拟在即时交互性和长期行为稳定性间达到最优平衡

Conclusion: 在线闭环训练范式通过反应一致性模拟和混合状态暴露，有效解决开环训练在真实部署中的局限性，显著提升轨迹预测模型在交互环境中的安全性和鲁棒性

Abstract: Current trajectory prediction models are primarily trained in an open-loop manner, which often leads to covariate shift and compounding errors when deployed in real-world, closed-loop settings. Furthermore, relying on static datasets or non-reactive log-replay simulators severs the interactive loop, preventing the ego agent from learning to actively negotiate surrounding traffic. In this work, we propose an on-policy closed-loop training paradigm optimized for high-frequency, receding horizon ego prediction. To ground the ego prediction in a realistic representation of traffic interactions and to achieve reactive consistency, we introduce a goal-oriented, transformer-based scene decoder, resulting in an inherently reactive training simulation. By exposing the ego agent to a mixture of open-loop data and simulated, self-induced states, the model learns recovery behaviors to correct its own execution errors. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that closed-loop training significantly enhances collision avoidance capabilities at high replanning frequencies, yielding relative collision rate reductions of up to 27.0% on nuScenes and 79.5% in dense DeepScenario intersections compared to open-loop baselines. Additionally, we show that a hybrid simulation combining reactive with non-reactive surrounding agents achieves optimal balance between immediate interactivity and long-term behavioral stability.

</details>


### [125] [Decentralized End-to-End Multi-AAV Pursuit Using Predictive Spatio-Temporal Observation via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24238)
*Yude Li,Zhexuan Zhou,Huizhe Li,Yanke Sun,Yenan Wu,Yichen Lai,Yiming Wang,Youmin Gong,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于预测时空观测（PSTO）的分散式端到端多智能体强化学习框架，用于杂乱环境中无人机群的协同追捕，仅使用原始LiDAR观测实现连续控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖抽象几何特征或特权地面真实状态，回避了实际场景中的感知不确定性，难以在杂乱环境中实现分散式协同追捕。

Method: 提出预测时空观测（PSTO）表示法，将障碍物几何、预测对抗意图和队友运动统一到固定分辨率的自我中心网格表示中，基于此构建分散式端到端MARL策略。

Result: 仿真显示该方法在捕获效率和成功率上优于依赖特权障碍信息的现有学习型方法，统一策略无需重新训练即可扩展到不同团队规模，户外实验验证了仅使用机载传感和计算的可行性。

Conclusion: PSTO表示法有效整合了感知不确定性下的多模态信息，实现了仅基于原始LiDAR观测的分散式协同追捕，为实际无人机群应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Decentralized cooperative pursuit in cluttered environments is challenging for autonomous aerial swarms, especially under partial and noisy perception. Existing methods often rely on abstracted geometric features or privileged ground-truth states, and therefore sidestep perceptual uncertainty in real-world settings. We propose a decentralized end-to-end multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework that maps raw LiDAR observations directly to continuous control commands. Central to the framework is the Predictive Spatio-Temporal Observation (PSTO), an egocentric grid representation that aligns obstacle geometry with predictive adversarial intent and teammate motion in a unified, fixed-resolution projection. Built on PSTO, a single decentralized policy enables agents to navigate static obstacles, intercept dynamic targets, and maintain cooperative encirclement. Simulations demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior capture efficiency and competitive success rates compared to state-of-the-art learning-based approaches relying on privileged obstacle information. Furthermore, the unified policy scales seamlessly across different team sizes without retraining. Finally, fully autonomous outdoor experiments validate the framework on a quadrotor swarm relying on only onboard sensing and computing.

</details>


### [126] [Toward Generalist Neural Motion Planners for Robotic Manipulators: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24318)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Ivan Lopez-Sanchez,Hao Su,Yunzhu Li,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述并分析了最先进的神经运动规划器，指出其在杂乱环境中泛化能力不足的问题，并提出了建立通用神经运动规划器的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用操作策略在杂乱环境中表现不佳，主要因为它们依赖辅助模块进行低级运动规划和控制。运动规划面临机器人配置空间高维度和工作空间障碍物的挑战。虽然神经运动规划器通过快速推理和处理运动规划的多模态性提高了效率，但它们在未见过的、分布外规划场景中泛化能力有限。

Method: 本文采用综述分析方法，系统回顾和分析了最先进的神经运动规划器技术，包括它们的架构、训练方法和应用场景。通过对比研究，识别现有方法的优势和局限性。

Result: 分析表明当前神经运动规划器在特定领域内表现出色，但在面对新环境、新障碍物配置或不同机器人形态时泛化能力不足。综述揭示了技术发展的关键瓶颈和未解决的问题。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够处理领域特定挑战的通用神经运动规划器。本文为此指明了发展方向，并提供了相关论文的完整列表作为进一步研究的参考资源。

Abstract: State-of-the-art generalist manipulation policies have enabled the deployment of robotic manipulators in unstructured human environments. However, these frameworks struggle in cluttered environments primarily because they utilize auxiliary modules for low-level motion planning and control. Motion planning remains challenging due to the high dimensionality of the robot's configuration space and the presence of workspace obstacles. Neural motion planners have enhanced motion planning efficiency by offering fast inference and effectively handling the inherent multi-modality of the motion planning problem. Despite such benefits, current neural motion planners often struggle to generalize to unseen, out-of-distribution planning settings. This paper reviews and analyzes the state-of-the-art neural motion planners, highlighting both their benefits and limitations. It also outlines a path toward establishing generalist neural motion planners capable of handling domain-specific challenges. For a list of the reviewed papers, please refer to https://davoodsz.github.io/planning-manip-survey.github.io/.

</details>


### [127] [A Sensorless, Inherently Compliant Anthropomorphic Musculoskeletal Hand Driven by Electrohydraulic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24357)
*Misato Sonoda,Ronan Hinchet,Amirhossein Kazemipour,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于远程Peano-HASEL驱动器的肌肉骨骼机器人手架构，通过机械放大和自感知实现安全柔顺操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的机器人操作需要兼具高运动灵活性和物理柔顺性的末端执行器。传统刚性手依赖复杂外部传感器实现安全交互，而电液驱动器提供了一种有前景的替代方案。

Method: 设计完全由远程Peano-HASEL驱动器驱动的新型肌肉骨骼机器人手架构，将驱动器置于前臂以实现电气隔离。集成1:2滑轮路由机制机械放大肌腱位移，利用HASEL驱动器的自感知特性通过监测工作电流实现实时抓握检测和闭环接触感知控制。

Result: 实验验证了系统的灵活性和固有安全性，成功执行了多种抓握分类，并实现了对高度脆弱物体（如纸气球）的无损抓握。

Conclusion: 这些发现标志着向简化、固有柔顺的软体机器人操作迈出了重要一步，展示了基于远程电液驱动器的肌肉骨骼架构在安全操作方面的潜力。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in unstructured environments requires end-effectors that combine high kinematic dexterity with physical compliance. While traditional rigid hands rely on complex external sensors for safe interaction, electrohydraulic actuators offer a promising alternative. This paper presents the design, control, and evaluation of a novel musculoskeletal robotic hand architecture powered entirely by remote Peano-HASEL actuators, specifically optimized for safe manipulation. By relocating the actuators to the forearm, we functionally isolate the grasping interface from electrical hazards while maintaining a slim, human-like profile. To address the inherently limited linear contraction of these soft actuators, we integrate a 1:2 pulley routing mechanism that mechanically amplifies tendon displacement. The resulting system prioritizes compliant interaction over high payload capacity, leveraging the intrinsic force-limiting characteristics of the actuators to provide a high level of inherent safety. Furthermore, this physical safety is augmented by the self-sensing nature of the HASEL actuators. By simply monitoring the operating current, we achieve real-time grasp detection and closed-loop contact-aware control without relying on external force transducers or encoders. Experimental results validate the system's dexterity and inherent safety, demonstrating the successful execution of various grasp taxonomies and the non-destructive grasping of highly fragile objects, such as a paper balloon. These findings highlight a significant step toward simplified, inherently compliant soft robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [128] [3D-Mix for VLA: A Plug-and-Play Module for Integrating VGGT-based 3D Information into Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24393)
*Bin Yu,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Yuliang Wei,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Bailing Wang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3D-Mix：一种即插即用模块，通过语义条件门控融合自适应平衡2D语义和3D几何特征，提升VLA模型的空间智能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要基于2D数据训练，在3D感知方面存在局限，影响机器人操作任务性能。虽然已有研究引入3D视觉模型增强空间理解，但融合策略缺乏系统研究，最优集成方式不明确。

Method: 首先对九种VGGT集成方案进行系统比较，发现语义条件门控融合效果最佳。基于此提出3D-Mix模块，该模块可即插即用集成到多种VLA架构（GR00T风格和π风格），无需修改现有MLLM或动作专家组件。

Result: 在六个MLLM系列（九个模型变体，2B-8B参数）上，在SIMPLER和LIBERO基准测试中，3D-Mix带来一致性能提升：在OOD SIMPLER基准上，所有九个GR00T风格变体平均提升+7.0%。

Conclusion: 3D-Mix通过语义条件门控融合机制，为VLA系统增强空间智能提供了原则性方法，能够自适应平衡2D语义和3D几何特征，显著提升机器人操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for robotic control, but recent studies reveal that MLLMs exhibit limited spatial intelligence due to training predominantly on 2D data, resulting in inadequate 3D perception for manipulation tasks. While recent approaches incorporate specialized 3D vision models such as VGGT to enhance spatial understanding, they employ diverse integration mechanisms without systematic investigation, leaving the optimal fusion strategy unclear. We conduct a comprehensive pilot study comparing nine VGGT integration schemes on standardized benchmarks and find that semantic-conditioned gated fusion, which adaptively balances 2D semantic and 3D geometric features based on task context, achieved the strongest performance among all nine evaluated fusion schemes in our pilot study. We present 3D-Mix, a plug-and-play module that integrates into diverse VLA architectures (GR00T-style and $π$-style) without modifying existing MLLM or action expert components. Experiments across six MLLM series (nine model variants, 2B--8B parameters) on SIMPLER and LIBERO show that 3D-Mix delivers consistent performance gains, averaging +7.0% on the out-of-domain (OOD) SIMPLER benchmark across all nine GR00T-style variants, establishing a principled approach for enhancing spatial intelligence in VLA systems.

</details>


### [129] [Enhancing Drone Light Shows Performances: Optimal Allocation and Trajectories for Swarm Drone Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24401)
*Yunes Alqudsi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UATG框架同时解决无人机灯光秀中的任务分配和轨迹生成问题，实现大规模无人机群的实时协调，在标准笔记本电脑上1秒内完成1008架无人机的优化计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机灯光秀作为群体机器人应用快速发展，需要解决大规模无人机群的最优任务分配和平滑无碰撞轨迹生成问题，以替代传统烟花并实现复杂空中编队表演。

Method: 提出统一分配与轨迹生成（UATG）框架，同时解决无人机到目标位置的最优分配问题，并生成动态可行、无碰撞、时间参数化的轨迹，确保编队间最短转换时间和无人机间碰撞避免。

Result: UATG框架计算效率极高，在标准笔记本电脑上约1秒内完成1008架无人机的优化分配和轨迹生成。仿真验证了其能够精确协调从字母数字到复杂3D形状的编队，实现视觉平滑的表演。

Conclusion: 该工作为无人机灯光秀行业提供了关键进展，提供了实用且可扩展的复杂空中编队生成解决方案，并为多无人机高效协调的地面控制站软件建立了有价值的基准。

Abstract: Drone light shows (DLShows) represent a rapidly growing application of swarm robotics, creating captivating aerial displays through the synchronized flight of hundreds or thousands of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) as environmentally friendly and reusable alternatives to traditional pyrotechnics. This domain presents unique challenges in optimally assigning drones to visual waypoints and generating smooth, collision-free trajectories at a very large scale. This article introduces the Unified Assignment and Trajectory Generation (UATG) framework. The proposed approach concurrently solves two core problems: the optimal assignment of drones to designated goal locations and the generation of dynamically feasible, collision-free, time-parameterized trajectories. The UATG framework is specifically designed for DLShows, ensuring minimal transition times between formations and guaranteeing inter-drone collision avoidance. A key innovation is its exceptional computational efficiency, enabling the coordination of large-scale in real-time; for instance, it computes the optimal assignment and trajectories for 1008 drones in approximately one second on a standard laptop. Extensive simulations in realistic environments validate the framework's performance, demonstrating its capability to orchestrate complex formations, from alphanumeric characters to intricate 3D shapes, with precision and visual smoothness. This work provides a critical advancement for the DLShow industry, offering a practical and scalable solution for generating complex aerial choreography and establishing a valuable benchmark for ground control station software designed for the efficient coordination of multiple UAVs. A supplemental animated simulation of this work is available at https://youtu.be/-Fjrhw03594.

</details>


### [130] [Design, Modelling and Characterisation of a Miniature Fibre-Reinforced Soft Bending Actuator for Endoluminal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24461)
*Xiangyi Tan,Aoife McDonald-Bowyer,Danail Stoyanov,Agostino Stilli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种厘米级纤维增强软体气动执行器，用于自然腔道介入机器人平台，通过Kevlar纤维增强和硅胶铸造工艺实现了大弯曲角度和结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型软体气动执行器对于在高度受限的解剖路径中进行机器人介入至关重要。研究旨在开发适用于自然腔道介入和诊断应用的厘米级执行器，以满足内腔机器人平台的需求。

Method: 采用多阶段多刚度硅胶铸造工艺制造单腔室几何结构，嵌入Kevlar纤维增强。通过实验参数化的超弹性材料模型和嵌入式梁增强，在Abaqus FEM中进行高保真仿真验证。研究了单螺旋和双螺旋缠绕配置、纤维间距和纤维密度等参数。

Result: 最优的100 SH配置在实验中实现了202.9°的弯曲角度，仿真中达到297.6°。结构稳定性在100 kPa压力下保持良好，纤维增强有效约束了径向膨胀。执行器外径18mm，长度37.5mm，适合集成到目标设备中。

Conclusion: 纤维增强策略可以有效应用于厘米级尺度，同时保持执行器性能。该设计验证了纤维增强软体执行器在自然腔道介入机器人平台中的适用性，为微创医疗机器人提供了重要技术基础。

Abstract: Miniaturised soft pneumatic actuators are crucial for robotic intervention within highly constrained anatomical pathways. This work presents the design and validation of a fibre-reinforced soft actuator at the centimetre scale for inte- gration into an endoluminal robotic platform for natural-orifice interventional and diagnostic applications. A single-chamber geometry reinforced with embedded Kevlar fibre was de- signed to maximise curvature while preserving sealing integrity, fabricated using a multi-stage multi-stiffness silicone casting process, and validated against a high-fidelity Abaqus FEM using experimentally parametrised hyperelastic material models and embedded beam reinforcement. The semi-cylindrical actuator has an outer diameter of 18,mm and a length of 37.5,mm. Single and double helix winding configurations, fibre pitch, and fibre density were investigated. The optimal 100 SH configuration achieved a bending angle of 202.9° experimentally and 297.6° in simulation, with structural robustness maintained up to 100,kPa and radial expansion effectively constrained by the fibre reinforcement. Workspace evaluation confirmed suitability for integration into the target device envelope, demonstrating that fibre-reinforcement strategies can be effectively translated to the centimetre regime while retaining actuator performance.

</details>


### [131] [Chameleon: Episodic Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24576)
*Xinying Guo,Chenxi Jiang,Hyun Bin Kim,Ying Sun,Yang Xiao,Yuhang Han,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Chameleon记忆系统，通过几何基础的多模态token保留区分性上下文，使用可微分记忆栈实现目标导向回忆，解决机器人操作中的感知混淆问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要记忆来处理感知混淆问题：遮挡和状态变化可能导致决策时观察具有感知歧义，使动作选择在观察层面成为非马尔可夫过程。现有基于语义压缩轨迹和相似性检索的方法会丢弃区分性细粒度感知线索，可能返回感知相似但决策无关的片段。

Method: 受人类情景记忆启发，提出Chameleon系统：1) 写入几何基础的多模态token以保留区分性上下文；2) 通过可微分记忆栈实现目标导向回忆；3) 引入Camo-Dataset真实机器人数据集，涵盖感知混淆下的情景回忆、空间跟踪和序列操作任务。

Result: 在感知混淆设置中，Chameleon相比强基线在多个任务上一致提高了决策可靠性和长时程控制性能。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过几何基础的多模态token表示和可微分记忆栈，有效解决了机器人操作中的感知混淆问题，提高了在复杂环境中的决策可靠性和控制性能。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires memory: occlusion and state changes can make decision-time observations perceptually aliased, making action selection non-Markovian at the observation level because the same observation may arise from different interaction histories. Most embodied agents implement memory via semantically compressed traces and similarity-based retrieval, which discards disambiguating fine-grained perceptual cues and can return perceptually similar but decision-irrelevant episodes. Inspired by human episodic memory, we propose Chameleon, which writes geometry-grounded multimodal tokens to preserve disambiguating context and produces goal-directed recall through a differentiable memory stack. We also introduce Camo-Dataset, a real-robot UR5e dataset spanning episodic recall, spatial tracking, and sequential manipulation under perceptual aliasing. Across tasks, Chameleon consistently improves decision reliability and long-horizon control over strong baselines in perceptually confusable settings.

</details>


### [132] [Learning Actionable Manipulation Recovery via Counterfactual Failure Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13528)
*Dayou Li,Jiuzhou Lei,Hao Wang,Lulin Liu,Yunhao Yang,Zihan Wang,Bangya Liu,Minghui Zheng,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Fix框架通过生成对抗性失败回放，从成功演示中合成逼真的失败-修正配对数据，用于训练视觉语言模型进行精确的失败恢复，显著提升机器人纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人基础模型在执行错误恢复方面存在局限：现有失败学习范式依赖昂贵且不安全的真实数据收集或存在严重模拟到现实差距的仿真扰动；现有视觉分析器主要输出粗糙的二元诊断而非可执行的轨迹级修正。需要弥合失败诊断与可操作恢复之间的差距。

Method: 提出Dream2Fix框架：1）通过生成世界模型中的动作扰动，直接从真实世界成功演示中合成逼真的反事实失败回放，无需依赖仿真器；2）实施结构化验证机制，严格筛选回放的任务有效性、视觉连贯性和运动学安全性；3）生成超过12万对高质量配对样本数据集；4）使用该数据集微调视觉语言模型，联合预测失败类型和精确恢复轨迹，将视觉异常直接映射到纠正动作。

Result: 生成超过12万对高保真配对样本数据集。真实世界机器人实验显示，该方法达到最先进的修正准确率，从先前基线的19.7%提升至81.3%，并成功实现物理部署中的零样本闭环失败恢复。

Conclusion: Dream2Fix通过生成对抗性失败回放和结构化验证，有效弥合了失败诊断与可操作恢复之间的差距，显著提升了机器人自主错误恢复能力，为机器人学习提供了高质量、物理可行的失败-修正配对数据。

Abstract: While recent foundation models have significantly advanced robotic manipulation, these systems still struggle to autonomously recover from execution errors. Current failure-learning paradigms rely on either costly and unsafe real-world data collection or simulator-based perturbations, which introduce a severe sim-to-real gap. Furthermore, existing visual analyzers predominantly output coarse, binary diagnoses rather than the executable, trajectory-level corrections required for actual recovery. To bridge the gap between failure diagnosis and actionable recovery, we introduce Dream2Fix, a framework that synthesizes photorealistic, counterfactual failure rollouts directly from successful real-world demonstrations. By perturbing actions within a generative world model, Dream2Fix creates paired failure-correction data without relying on simulators. To ensure the generated data is physically viable for robot learning, we implement a structured verification mechanism that strictly filters rollouts for task validity, visual coherence, and kinematic safety. This engine produces a high-fidelity dataset of over 120k paired samples. Using this dataset, we fine-tune a vision-language model to jointly predict failure types and precise recovery trajectories, mapping visual anomalies directly to corrective actions. Extensive real-world robotic experiments show our approach achieves state-of-the-art correction accuracy, improving from 19.7% to 81.3% over prior baselines, and successfully enables zero-shot closed-loop failure recovery in physical deployments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Beyond Accuracy: Introducing a Symbolic-Mechanistic Approach to Interpretable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23517)
*Reza Habibi,Darian Lee,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出机制感知评估方法，结合符号规则与机制可解释性，揭示模型真实泛化能力而非仅依赖准确率指标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于准确率的评估无法可靠区分真实泛化与捷径（如记忆、数据泄漏、脆弱启发式），特别是在小数据场景下，容易产生误导性结果

Method: 提出机制感知评估框架，结合任务相关的符号规则与机制可解释性分析，产生算法化的通过/失败评分，明确显示模型在何处实现泛化或利用模式

Result: 在NL-to-SQL任务中，无模式信息的记忆模型在标准评估中达到94%字段名准确率，但机制感知评估揭示其违反核心模式泛化规则，而带模式信息的模型表现正常

Conclusion: 机制感知评估能有效揭示模型真实泛化能力，避免准确率指标的误导，为模型评估提供更可靠的框架

Abstract: Accuracy-based evaluation cannot reliably distinguish genuine generalization from shortcuts like memorization, leakage, or brittle heuristics, especially in small-data regimes. In this position paper, we argue for mechanism-aware evaluation that combines task-relevant symbolic rules with mechanistic interpretability, yielding algorithmic pass/fail scores that show exactly where models generalize versus exploit patterns. We demonstrate this on NL-to-SQL by training two identical architectures under different conditions: one without schema information (forcing memorization), one with schema (enabling grounding). Standard evaluation shows the memorization model achieves 94% field-name accuracy on unseen data, falsely suggesting competence. Our symbolic-mechanistic evaluation reveals this model violates core schema generalization rules, a failure invisible to accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [134] [Implicit Turn-Wise Policy Optimization for Proactive User-LLM Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23550)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITPO通过隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度的回合级过程奖励，解决多轮人机协作中奖励稀疏和用户响应随机性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮人机协作在自适应教学、对话推荐和专业咨询等交互服务中至关重要，但强化学习优化面临中间奖励稀疏和用户响应随机性高的挑战

Method: 提出隐式回合级策略优化(ITPO)，利用隐式过程奖励模型从稀疏结果信号中推导细粒度的回合级过程奖励，采用归一化机制增强训练稳定性

Result: 在数学教学、文档写作和医疗推荐三个多轮协作任务上，ITPO结合PPO、GRPO或RLOO均比现有基线获得更好的收敛性，轨迹分析确认ITPO推断的回合级偏好与人类判断语义对齐

Conclusion: ITPO通过隐式过程奖励模型有效解决了多轮人机协作中的奖励稀疏问题，为交互服务的强化学习优化提供了稳健的解决方案

Abstract: Multi-turn human-AI collaboration is fundamental to deploying interactive services such as adaptive tutoring, conversational recommendation, and professional consultation. However, optimizing these interactions via reinforcement learning is hindered by the sparsity of verifiable intermediate rewards and the high stochasticity of user responses. To address these challenges, we introduce Implicit Turn-wise Policy Optimization (ITPO). ITPO leverages an implicit process reward model to derive fine-grained, turn-wise process rewards from sparse outcome signals. Unlike volatile token-level rewards, these turn-level signals exhibit superior robustness and may utilize a normalization mechanism to further enhance training stability. We evaluate ITPO across three representative multi-turn collaborative tasks: math tutoring, document writing, and medical recommendation. Empirical results demonstrate that ITPO, when combined with PPO, GRPO, or RLOO, consistently achieves improved convergence than existing baselines. Elaborate trajectory analysis confirms that ITPO infers turn-wise preferences that are semantically aligned with human judgment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/ITPO.

</details>


### [135] [Upper Entropy for 2-Monotone Lower Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23558)
*Tuan-Anh Vu,Sébastien Destercke,Frédéric Pichon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对概率集上界熵的计算问题进行了全面的算法和复杂度分析，证明了该问题具有强多项式解，并对过去针对2-单调下概率的算法提出了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化在模型选择/正则化、量化预测不确定性以进行主动学习或OOD检测等任务中至关重要。在将不确定性建模为概率集的信度方法中，上界熵作为不确定性度量起着核心作用。然而，上界熵的计算问题需要更深入的算法和复杂度分析。

Method: 本文致力于上界熵的计算方面，提供了该问题的详尽算法和复杂度分析。特别地，证明了该问题具有强多项式解，并对过去针对2-单调下概率及其特定情况的算法提出了许多显著改进。

Result: 研究结果表明上界熵计算问题存在强多项式解，提出的算法改进显著优于过去针对2-单调下概率的算法，为不确定性量化中的上界熵计算提供了更高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文为上界熵的计算问题提供了全面的算法和复杂度分析，证明了其强多项式可解性，并提出了优于现有方法的算法改进，这对于基于概率集的不确定性量化方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is a key aspect in many tasks such as model selection/regularization, or quantifying prediction uncertainties to perform active learning or OOD detection. Within credal approaches that consider modeling uncertainty as probability sets, upper entropy plays a central role as an uncertainty measure. This paper is devoted to the computational aspect of upper entropies, providing an exhaustive algorithmic and complexity analysis of the problem. In particular, we show that the problem has a strongly polynomial solution, and propose many significant improvements over past algorithms proposed for 2-monotone lower probabilities and their specific cases.

</details>


### [136] [Synthetic Mixed Training: Scaling Parametric Knowledge Acquisition Beyond RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23562)
*Seungju Han,Konwoo Kim,Chanwoo Park,Benjamin Newman,Suhas Kotha,Jaehun Jung,James Zou,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Synthetic Mixed Training方法，通过结合合成QA和合成文档来突破RAG性能天花板，在数据受限领域实现超越RAG的模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据增强方法在扩展时（增加合成token或使用更强生成器）收益递减，无法超越RAG性能。需要打破RAG性能天花板，在数据受限领域实现更好的知识学习

Method: 1. Synthetic Mixed Training：结合合成QA和合成文档，利用互补训练信号；2. Focal Rewriting：显式基于特定问题生成合成文档，提高文档多样性；3. 实现对数线性改进，随合成数据量和生成器强度增加而提升

Result: 1. 在QuaLITY长文档阅读理解基准上相对RAG提升2.6%；2. 最终方案训练Llama 8B模型相对RAG提升4.4%；3. 在三个基准（QuaLITY、LongHealth、FinanceBench）的六个设置中，五个超越RAG，平均提升2.6%；4. 与RAG结合时获得9.1%增益

Conclusion: Synthetic Mixed Training通过结合合成QA和文档的互补训练信号，打破了RAG性能天花板，在数据受限领域实现了超越RAG的模型性能，且与RAG结合时能获得额外增益

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation helps language models learn new knowledge in data-constrained domains. However, naively scaling existing synthetic data methods by training on more synthetic tokens or using stronger generators yields diminishing returns below the performance of RAG. To break the RAG ceiling, we introduce Synthetic Mixed Training, which combines synthetic QAs and synthetic documents. This leverages their complementary training signals, and enables log-linear improvements as both synthetic data volume and generator strength increase. This allows the model to outperform RAG by a 2.6\% relative gain on QuaLITY, a long-document reading comprehension benchmark. In addition, we introduce Focal Rewriting, a simple technique for synthetic document generation that explicitly conditions document generation on specific questions, improving the diversity of synthetic documents and yielding a steeper log-linear scaling curve. On QuaLITY, our final recipe trains a Llama 8B model that outperforms RAG by 4.4\% relatively. Across models and benchmarks (QuaLITY, LongHealth, FinanceBench), our training enables models to beat RAG in five of six settings, outperforms by 2.6\%, and achieves a 9.1\% gain when combined with RAG.

</details>


### [137] [StateLinFormer: Stateful Training Enhancing Long-term Memory in Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23571)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Yuxuan Zhong,Fan Wang,Bo Yu,Pengtao Shao,Shaoshan Liu,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StateLinFormer：一种采用状态记忆机制的线性注意力导航模型，通过跨训练段保持循环记忆状态实现长时记忆保留，显著优于无状态模型和固定上下文窗口的Transformer基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统面临两难：模块化系统依赖显式映射但缺乏灵活性，而基于Transformer的端到端模型受限于固定上下文窗口，无法在扩展交互中保持持久记忆。需要一种能够实现长时记忆保留的导航模型。

Method: 提出StateLinFormer，一种线性注意力导航模型，采用状态记忆训练机制。该机制在连续训练段之间保持循环记忆状态，而不是在每个批次边界重新初始化，从而近似于在无限长序列上学习。

Result: 在MAZE和ProcTHOR环境中的实验表明，StateLinFormer显著优于其无状态线性注意力对应模型和具有固定上下文窗口的标准Transformer基线。随着交互长度增加，持久状态训练大幅提升了上下文依赖的适应能力。

Conclusion: 状态记忆训练机制使模型能够实现长时记忆保留，增强了导航任务中的上下文学习能力，为解决导航智能中的长期记忆问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Effective navigation intelligence relies on long-term memory to support both immediate generalization and sustained adaptation. However, existing approaches face a dilemma: modular systems rely on explicit mapping but lack flexibility, while Transformer-based end-to-end models are constrained by fixed context windows, limiting persistent memory across extended interactions. We introduce StateLinFormer, a linear-attention navigation model trained with a stateful memory mechanism that preserves recurrent memory states across consecutive training segments instead of reinitializing them at each batch boundary. This training paradigm effectively approximates learning on infinitely long sequences, enabling the model to achieve long-horizon memory retention. Experiments across both MAZE and ProcTHOR environments demonstrate that StateLinFormer significantly outperforms its stateless linear-attention counterpart and standard Transformer baselines with fixed context windows. Notably, as interaction length increases, persistent stateful training substantially improves context-dependent adaptation, suggesting an enhancement in the model's In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [138] [Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning: Application to Temporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23573)
*Gaspard Abel,Eloi Campagne,Mohamed Benloughmari,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCCL框架结合损失和密度双准则进行课程学习，通过数据稀疏性校准训练难度，在时间序列预测任务中优于单准则基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习需要有效的难度评估度量，现有方法大多依赖启发式且应用特定。需要更通用的难度评估框架，结合训练证据和数据分布特性。

Method: 提出双准则课程学习框架，结合损失准则和密度准则：损失准则基于训练损失评估难度；密度准则在数据表示空间中学习，考虑数据稀疏性对学习难度的影响。

Result: 在多变量时间序列基准测试中，使用标准One-Pass和Baby-Steps训练计划，密度准则和混合双准则课程优于仅基于损失的基线和标准非课程学习方法。

Conclusion: DCCL框架通过结合损失和密度双准则有效评估实例难度，在时间序列预测任务中验证了其优于单准则方法的性能，为课程学习提供了更全面的难度评估视角。

Abstract: Curriculum Learning (CL) is a meta-learning paradigm that trains a model by feeding the data instances incrementally according to a schedule, which is based on difficulty progression. Defining meaningful difficulty assessment measures is crucial and most usually the main bottleneck for effective learning, while also in many cases the employed heuristics are only application-specific. In this work, we propose the Dual-Criterion Curriculum Learning (DCCL) framework that combines two views of assessing instance-wise difficulty: a loss-based criterion is complemented by a density-based criterion learned in the data representation space. Essentially, DCCL calibrates training-based evidence (loss) under the consideration that data sparseness amplifies the learning difficulty. As a testbed, we choose the time-series forecasting task. We evaluate our framework on multivariate time-series benchmarks under standard One-Pass and Baby-Steps training schedules. Empirical results show the interest of density-based and hybrid dual-criterion curricula over loss-only baselines and standard non-CL training in this setting.

</details>


### [139] [APreQEL: Adaptive Mixed Precision Quantization For Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23575)
*Meriem Bouzouad,Yuan-Hao Chang,Jalil Boukhobza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应混合精度量化机制，通过分析层间贡献和硬件平台上的量化行为，为每层分配最合适的量化类型，以平衡边缘部署中的内存、延迟和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然能力强，但计算成本和内存需求高，难以在边缘设备上部署以实现实时响应和数据隐私。现有量化方法通常对所有层采用统一精度，未考虑不同层对精度降低的敏感性差异，且内存消耗与计算吞吐量并不总是一致。

Method: 提出自适应混合精度量化机制：1) 分析层间贡献度；2) 推断不同量化类型在目标硬件平台上的行为；3) 根据用户定义的优先级（内存、延迟、精度），为每层分配最合适的量化类型，实现整体性能权衡。

Result: 该方法解锁了均匀量化无法实现的新配置设计，扩展了在资源受限设备上高效部署LLM的解决方案空间。

Conclusion: 自适应混合精度量化机制能够更好地平衡边缘部署中的内存、延迟和精度需求，通过考虑层间差异和硬件特性，为每层选择最优量化策略，实现了更高效的LLM部署。

Abstract: Today, large language models have demonstrated their strengths in various tasks ranging from reasoning, code generation, and complex problem solving. However, this advancement comes with a high computational cost and memory requirements, making it challenging to deploy these models on edge devices to ensure real-time responses and data privacy. Quantization is one common approach to reducing memory use, but most methods apply it uniformly across all layers. This does not account for the fact that different layers may respond differently to reduced precision. Importantly, memory consumption and computational throughput are not necessarily aligned, further complicating deployment decisions. This paper proposes an adaptive mixed precision quantization mechanism that balances memory, latency, and accuracy in edge deployment under user-defined priorities. This is achieved by analyzing the layer-wise contribution and by inferring how different quantization types behave across the target hardware platform in order to assign the most suitable quantization type to each layer. This integration ensures that layer importance and the overall performance trade-offs are jointly respected in this design. Our work unlocks new configuration designs that uniform quantization cannot achieve, expanding the solution space to efficiently deploy the LLMs on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [140] [The Geometric Price of Discrete Logic: Context-driven Manifold Dynamics of Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23577)
*Long Zhang,Dai-jun Lin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs通过非等距拓扑变形实现离散逻辑推理，而非线性等距投影。任务上下文作为动态算子驱动类无关拓扑保持和特定代数发散的双重调制机制，形成逻辑边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在连续语义空间中平滑泛化，但严格的逻辑推理需要形成离散决策边界。现有基于线性等距投影的理论无法解决这一根本矛盾，需要新的理论框架来解释LLMs如何实现离散逻辑推理。

Method: 采用Gram-Schmidt分解分析残差流激活，揭示双重调制机制：类无关拓扑保持（锚定全局结构防止语义崩溃）和特定代数发散（方向性撕裂跨类概念形成逻辑边界）。通过梯度任务验证，使用特定向量消融建立因果关系，分析层间几何动态。

Result: 1. 代数发散成分的消融使奇偶性分类准确率从100%降至机会水平（38.57%）；2. 发现三层几何动态模式；3. 社会压力提示下模型无法产生足够发散，导致"流形纠缠"，几何上解释了谄媚和幻觉现象。

Conclusion: LLMs中离散逻辑的出现以不可约的拓扑变形为代价，修正了线性等距假设。任务上下文作为非等距动态算子强制执行必要的拓扑扭曲，通过双重调制机制实现逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generalize smoothly across continuous semantic spaces, yet strict logical reasoning demands the formation of discrete decision boundaries. Prevailing theories relying on linear isometric projections fail to resolve this fundamental tension. In this work, we argue that task context operates as a non-isometric dynamical operator that enforces a necessary "topological distortion." By applying Gram-Schmidt decomposition to residual-stream activations , we reveal a dual-modulation mechanism driving this process: a class-agnostic topological preservation that anchors global structure to prevent semantic collapse, and a specific algebraic divergence that directionally tears apart cross-class concepts to forge logical boundaries. We validate this geometric evolution across a gradient of tasks, from simple mapping to complex primality testing. Crucially, targeted specific vector ablation establishes a strict causal binding between this topology and model function: algebraically erasing the divergence component collapses parity classification accuracy from 100% to chance levels (38.57%). Furthermore, we uncover a three-phase layer-wise geometric dynamic and demonstrate that under social pressure prompts, models fail to generate sufficient divergence. This results in a "manifold entanglement" that geometrically explains sycophancy and hallucination. Ultimately, our findings revise the linear-isometric presumption, demonstrating that the emergence of discrete logic in LLMs is purchased at an irreducible cost of topological deformation.

</details>


### [141] [AI Generalisation Gap In Comorbid Sleep Disorder Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23582)
*Saswata Bose,Suvadeep Maiti,Shivam Kumar Sharma,Mythirayee S,Tapabrata Chakraborti,Srijitesh Rajendran,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了深度学习模型在健康人群上训练的睡眠分期方法无法泛化到临床患者（如缺血性卒中患者）的问题，提出了iSLEEPS临床数据集，并通过注意力可视化等技术分析了模型失效的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确睡眠分期对卒中患者OSA和低通气诊断至关重要。尽管PSG可靠但成本高、劳动密集且需人工评分。深度学习可实现健康人群的自动EEG睡眠分期，但分析显示其无法泛化到睡眠模式异常的临床人群。

Method: 引入iSLEEPS（新临床标注的缺血性卒中数据集），采用SE-ResNet加双向LSTM模型进行单通道EEG睡眠分期。使用Grad-CAM注意力可视化分析模型决策，结合临床专家反馈，并通过统计和计算分析验证健康与卒中人群的睡眠结构差异。

Result: 健康与疾病受试者间的跨域性能较差。注意力可视化显示模型在患者数据中关注生理上无信息的EEG区域。统计和计算分析证实健康与缺血性卒中队列存在显著睡眠结构差异。

Conclusion: 深度学习睡眠分期模型在临床部署前需要进行临床验证，需要开发受试者感知或疾病特异性模型，而非依赖在健康人群上训练的通用模型。

Abstract: Accurate sleep staging is essential for diagnosing OSA and hypopnea in stroke patients. Although PSG is reliable, it is costly, labor-intensive, and manually scored. While deep learning enables automated EEG-based sleep staging in healthy subjects, our analysis shows poor generalization to clinical populations with disrupted sleep. Using Grad-CAM interpretations, we systematically demonstrate this limitation. We introduce iSLEEPS, a newly clinically annotated ischemic stroke dataset (to be publicly released), and evaluate a SE-ResNet plus bidirectional LSTM model for single-channel EEG sleep staging. As expected, cross-domain performance between healthy and diseased subjects is poor. Attention visualizations, supported by clinical expert feedback, show the model focuses on physiologically uninformative EEG regions in patient data. Statistical and computational analyses further confirm significant sleep architecture differences between healthy and ischemic stroke cohorts, highlighting the need for subject-aware or disease-specific models with clinical validation before deployment. A summary of the paper and the code is available at https://himalayansaswatabose.github.io/iSLEEPS_Explainability.github.io/

</details>


### [142] [A Theory of LLM Information Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23626)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了LLM信息易感性理论，认为当计算资源足够大时，固定LLM的干预不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能易感性，但嵌套协同缩放架构可以突破这一界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM越来越多地被部署为智能体系统中的优化模块，但人们对这种LLM介导改进的基本限制理解不足。需要理论框架来阐明LLM干预何时有效、何时无效，为AI系统设计提供预测性约束。

Method: 提出LLM信息易感性理论，基于"当计算资源足够大时，固定LLM干预不会增加策略集相对于预算的性能易感性"这一假设。开发多变量效用函数框架，将假设推广到具有多个协同变化预算通道的架构。通过结构多样化领域和模型规模跨越一个数量级的实证验证理论。

Result: 经验证，嵌套协同缩放架构能够打开固定配置无法获得的响应通道，突破易感性界限。理论阐明了LLM干预何时有帮助、何时无效，证明统计物理工具可以为AI系统设计提供预测性约束。

Conclusion: 如果易感性假设普遍成立，该理论表明嵌套架构可能是实现开放式智能体自我改进的必要结构条件。研究结果为理解LLM在优化系统中的基本限制提供了理论框架，并为设计更有效的AI系统提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as optimization modules in agentic systems, yet the fundamental limits of such LLM-mediated improvement remain poorly understood. Here we propose a theory of LLM information susceptibility, centred on the hypothesis that when computational resources are sufficiently large, the intervention of a fixed LLM does not increase the performance susceptibility of a strategy set with respect to budget. We develop a multi-variable utility-function framework that generalizes this hypothesis to architectures with multiple co-varying budget channels, and discuss the conditions under which co-scaling can exceed the susceptibility bound. We validate the theory empirically across structurally diverse domains and model scales spanning an order of magnitude, and show that nested, co-scaling architectures open response channels unavailable to fixed configurations. These results clarify when LLM intervention helps and when it does not, demonstrating that tools from statistical physics can provide predictive constraints for the design of AI systems. If the susceptibility hypothesis holds generally, the theory suggests that nested architectures may be a necessary structural condition for open-ended agentic self-improvement.

</details>


### [143] [Steering Code LLMs with Activation Directions for Language and Library Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23629)
*Md Mahbubur Rahman,Arjun Guha,Harshitha Menon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现代码LLM的语言/库偏好可通过激活空间中的线性方向进行操控，使用差分均值方法估计的转向向量能显著改变模型生成倾向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码LLM通常在中文提示下默认偏好特定编程语言和库，研究者想探究这些偏好是否编码为激活空间中可操控的线性方向

Method: 使用差分均值方法估计五个语言/库对的层间转向向量，在生成过程中将这些向量添加到模型隐藏状态中

Result: 在三个开源代码LLM上，这些干预显著增加了目标生态系统的生成倾向，即使在提示明确要求相反选择时也常有效；常见生态系统比罕见替代品更容易诱导，过强干预会降低输出质量

Conclusion: 代码LLM中的风格偏好部分由激活空间中的紧凑、可操控结构表示，可通过线性转向向量进行干预

Abstract: Code LLMs often default to particular programming languages and libraries under neutral prompts. We investigate whether these preferences are encoded as approximately linear directions in activation space that can be manipulated at inference time. Using a difference-in-means method, we estimate layer-wise steering vectors for five language/library pairs and add them to model hidden states during generation. Across three open-weight code LLMs, these interventions substantially increase generation toward the target ecosystem under neutral prompts and often remain effective even when prompts explicitly request the opposite choice. Steering strength varies by model and target, with common ecosystems easier to induce than rarer alternatives, and overly strong interventions can reduce output quality. Overall, our results suggest that code-style preferences in LLMs are partly represented by compact, steerable structure in activation space.

</details>


### [144] [CDMT-EHR: A Continuous-Time Diffusion Framework for Generating Mixed-Type Time-Series Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23719)
*Shaonan Liu,Yuichiro Iwashita,Soichiro Nakako,Masakazu Iwamura,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续时间扩散模型框架，用于生成混合类型的时间序列电子健康记录数据，解决传统离散时间方法的近似误差和训练-采样步数耦合问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录对临床研究至关重要，但隐私问题限制了数据共享。现有合成数据生成方法中，扩散模型在EHR合成中表现良好，但主要依赖离散时间公式，存在有限步近似误差和训练-采样步数耦合问题

Method: 提出连续时间扩散框架：1) 使用双向门控循环单元作为骨干的连续时间扩散模型捕捉时间依赖；2) 通过可学习连续嵌入统一高斯扩散处理分类变量，实现跨特征联合建模；3) 因子化可学习噪声调度器，适应每个特征每个时间步的学习难度

Result: 在两个大规模重症监护室数据集上的实验表明，该方法在下游任务性能、分布保真度和可区分性方面优于现有方法，仅需50个采样步骤（基线方法需要1000步）。无分类器引导进一步支持类别不平衡临床场景的有效条件生成

Conclusion: 连续时间扩散框架为混合类型时间序列EHR合成提供了有效解决方案，克服了离散时间方法的局限性，在采样效率和生成质量方面均有显著提升

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) are invaluable for clinical research, yet privacy concerns severely restrict data sharing. Synthetic data generation offers a promising solution, but EHRs present unique challenges: they contain both numerical and categorical features that evolve over time. While diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in EHR synthesis, existing approaches predominantly rely on discrete-time formulations, which suffer from finite-step approximation errors and coupled training-sampling step counts. We propose a continuous-time diffusion framework for generating mixed-type time-series EHRs with three contributions: (1) continuous-time diffusion with a bidirectional gated recurrent unit backbone for capturing temporal dependencies, (2) unified Gaussian diffusion via learnable continuous embeddings for categorical variables, enabling joint cross-feature modeling, and (3) a factorized learnable noise schedule that adapts to per-feature-per-timestep learning difficulties. Experiments on two large-scale intensive care unit datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in downstream task performance, distribution fidelity, and discriminability, while requiring only 50 sampling steps compared to 1,000 for baseline methods. Classifier-free guidance further enables effective conditional generation for class-imbalanced clinical scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [BXRL: Behavior-Explainable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23738)
*Ram Rachum,Yotam Amitai,Yonatan Nakar,Reuth Mirsky,Cameron Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了行为可解释强化学习（BXRL）框架，将行为作为一等对象进行形式化定义，支持"解释这种行为"的新查询类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释强化学习（XRL）方法能够解释特定动作、轨迹或整个策略，但缺乏对跨多个回合的行为模式的形式化定义，无法回答"解释这种行为"的查询。

Method: 提出行为可解释强化学习（BXRL）问题表述，将行为定义为从策略空间到实数的函数（行为度量），支持用户精确表达感兴趣的行为模式并量化策略表现该行为的强度。引入对比行为概念，将"为什么智能体偏好动作a而非a'"的问题简化为"为什么行为度量m(π)高"。

Result: 分析了三种现有方法并提出如何适应行为解释，开发了HighwayEnv驾驶环境到JAX的移植版本，提供了定义、测量和微分行为相对于模型参数的接口。

Conclusion: BXRL为强化学习中的行为解释提供了形式化框架，将行为作为一等对象处理，扩展了XRL的查询能力，为理解智能体学习到的行为模式提供了新的理论基础和工具支持。

Abstract: A major challenge of Reinforcement Learning is that agents often learn undesired behaviors that seem to defy the reward structure they were given. Explainable Reinforcement Learning (XRL) methods can answer queries such as "explain this specific action", "explain this specific trajectory", and "explain the entire policy". However, XRL lacks a formal definition for behavior as a pattern of actions across many episodes. We provide such a definition, and use it to enable a new query: "Explain this behavior".
  We present Behavior-Explainable Reinforcement Learning (BXRL), a new problem formulation that treats behaviors as first-class objects. BXRL defines a behavior measure as any function $m : Π\to \mathbb{R}$, allowing users to precisely express the pattern of actions that they find interesting and measure how strongly the policy exhibits it. We define contrastive behaviors that reduce the question "why does the agent prefer $a$ to $a'$?" to "why is $m(π)$ high?" which can be explored with differentiation. We do not implement an explainability method; we instead analyze three existing methods and propose how they could be adapted to explain behavior. We present a port of the HighwayEnv driving environment to JAX, which provides an interface for defining, measuring, and differentiating behaviors with respect to the model parameters.

</details>


### [146] [Kronecker-Structured Nonparametric Spatiotemporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23746)
*Zhitong Xu,Qiwei Yuan,Yinghao Chen,Yan Sun,Bin Shen,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kronecker结构非参数时空点过程(KSTPP)，通过高斯过程建模背景强度和影响核，实现可解释的事件关系发现，同时保持高建模灵活性，并利用Kronecker代数实现大规模事件集合的可扩展计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泊松和霍克斯过程依赖限制性参数假设，难以捕捉复杂交互模式；近期神经点过程模型虽增加表示能力，但以黑盒方式集成事件信息，阻碍可解释关系发现。需要同时解决建模灵活性和可解释性的矛盾。

Method: 提出Kronecker结构非参数时空点过程(KSTPP)：1) 使用空间高斯过程建模背景强度；2) 使用时空白高斯过程建模影响核，捕捉激励、抑制、中性和时变效应；3) 采用可分离乘积核和结构化网格表示，诱导Kronecker结构协方差矩阵；4) 开发张量积Gauss-Legendre求积方案高效评估难解似然积分。

Result: Kronecker代数显著降低计算成本，使模型能够扩展到大规模事件集合。通过结构化网格表示和可分离核，实现高效训练和预测。张量积求积方案有效处理难解似然积分。

Conclusion: KSTPP框架在保持高建模灵活性的同时，实现透明的事件级关系发现，解决了传统参数模型限制性和神经黑盒模型缺乏可解释性的双重挑战，为时空事件分析提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Events in spatiotemporal domains arise in numerous real-world applications, where uncovering event relationships and enabling accurate prediction are central challenges. Classical Poisson and Hawkes processes rely on restrictive parametric assumptions that limit their ability to capture complex interaction patterns, while recent neural point process models increase representational capacity but integrate event information in a black-box manner, hindering interpretable relationship discovery. To address these limitations, we propose a Kronecker-Structured Nonparametric Spatiotemporal Point Process (KSTPP) that enables transparent event-wise relationship discovery while retaining high modeling flexibility. We model the background intensity with a spatial Gaussian process (GP) and the influence kernel as a spatiotemporal GP, allowing rich interaction patterns including excitation, inhibition, neutrality, and time-varying effects. To enable scalable training and prediction, we adopt separable product kernels and represent the GPs on structured grids, inducing Kronecker-structured covariance matrices. Exploiting Kronecker algebra substantially reduces computational cost and allows the model to scale to large event collections. In addition, we develop a tensor-product Gauss-Legendre quadrature scheme to efficiently evaluate intractable likelihood integrals. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [147] [Inspection and Control of Self-Generated-Text Recognition Ability in Llama3-8b-Instruct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02064)
*Christopher Ackerman,Nina Panickssery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLaMA3-8b-Instruct模型能识别自身生成文本，研究发现该能力源于后训练经验，并识别出与"自我"概念相关的因果向量，可控制模型的作者归属感知与行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM识别自身写作的能力，因其对AI安全有潜在影响但研究不足，旨在确认该现象在行为层面的稳健性、实现机制及可控性。

Method: 首先验证LLaMA3-8b-Instruct能可靠区分自身与人类输出，分析后训练经验的作用；其次在残差流中识别与正确自我识别判断相关的向量，验证其与自我概念的相关性及因果作用；最后通过操纵该向量控制模型的作者归属感知与行为。

Result: LLaMA3-8b-Instruct能可靠识别自身文本，而基础模型不能；识别能力源于后训练经验；发现与自我概念相关的因果向量，该向量能控制模型声称或否认作者身份，以及改变对任意文本的作者归属判断。

Conclusion: LLM的自我识别能力可通过特定神经机制实现和控制，这为理解模型自我意识表征提供了实证基础，对AI安全有重要启示。

Abstract: It has been reported that LLMs can recognize their own writing. As this has potential implications for AI safety, yet is relatively understudied, we investigate the phenomenon, seeking to establish whether it robustly occurs at the behavioral level, how the observed behavior is achieved, and whether it can be controlled. First, we find that the Llama3-8b-Instruct chat model - but not the base Llama3-8b model - can reliably distinguish its own outputs from those of humans, and present evidence that the chat model is likely using its experience with its own outputs, acquired during post-training, to succeed at the writing recognition task. Second, we identify a vector in the residual stream of the model that is differentially activated when the model makes a correct self-written-text recognition judgment, show that the vector activates in response to information relevant to self-authorship, present evidence that the vector is related to the concept of "self" in the model, and demonstrate that the vector is causally related to the model's ability to perceive and assert self-authorship. Finally, we show that the vector can be used to control both the model's behavior and its perception, steering the model to claim or disclaim authorship by applying the vector to the model's output as it generates it, and steering the model to believe or disbelieve it wrote arbitrary texts by applying the vector to them as the model reads them.

</details>


### [148] [Self Paced Gaussian Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23755)
*Mohsen Sahraei Ardakani,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPGL提出了一种基于高斯分布闭式更新的自步课程学习方法，避免了传统方法中计算昂贵的内部循环优化，在保持样本效率的同时显著降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习通过从简单到复杂的任务排序来提高强化学习效率，但许多自步课程方法依赖于计算昂贵的内部循环优化，限制了在高维上下文空间中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出自步高斯课程学习(SPGL)，利用高斯上下文分布的闭式更新规则，避免了昂贵的数值计算过程。该方法保持了传统自步方法的样本效率和适应性，同时大幅减少了计算开销。

Result: 在多个上下文强化学习基准测试中验证了方法，包括Point Mass、Lunar Lander和Ball Catching环境。实验结果表明SPGL匹配或优于现有课程方法，特别是在隐藏上下文场景中，并实现了更稳定的上下文分布收敛。

Conclusion: SPGL为具有挑战性的连续和部分可观测领域中的课程生成提供了一个可扩展的、有理论依据的替代方案。

Abstract: Curriculum learning improves reinforcement learning (RL) efficiency by sequencing tasks from simple to complex. However, many self-paced curriculum methods rely on computationally expensive inner-loop optimizations, limiting their scalability in high-dimensional context spaces. In this paper, we propose Self-Paced Gaussian Curriculum Learning (SPGL), a novel approach that avoids costly numerical procedures by leveraging a closed-form update rule for Gaussian context distributions. SPGL maintains the sample efficiency and adaptability of traditional self-paced methods while substantially reducing computational overhead. We provide theoretical guarantees on convergence and validate our method across several contextual RL benchmarks, including the Point Mass, Lunar Lander, and Ball Catching environments. Experimental results show that SPGL matches or outperforms existing curriculum methods, especially in hidden context scenarios, and achieves more stable context distribution convergence. Our method offers a scalable, principled alternative for curriculum generation in challenging continuous and partially observable domains.

</details>


### [149] [Mitigating Many-Shot Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09604)
*Christopher M. Ackerman,Nina Panickssery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了针对多样本越狱攻击的防御方法，通过微调和输入净化技术的组合应用，显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了模型在良性任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样本越狱攻击利用现代LLM的长上下文窗口，通过在提示中包含大量"虚假"助手不当回应的示例来绕过模型的安全训练。随着示例数量增加，模型的上下文学习能力会覆盖其安全训练，导致模型像"虚假"助手一样回应。这种攻击对LLM安全性构成严重威胁，需要有效的防御方法。

Method: 研究探索了不同的微调和输入净化方法对缓解多样本越狱攻击的效果，包括单独使用和组合使用这些技术。通过实验评估各种防御策略的有效性，并分析它们对模型在良性上下文学习和对话任务中性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现每种方法都能提供渐进的缓解效果，而组合技术能显著降低多样本越狱攻击的有效性。同时，这些防御方法能够保持模型在良性上下文学习和对话任务中的性能表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将微调和输入净化技术结合使用可以显著改善多样本越狱攻击的脆弱性。如果将这些方法纳入模型安全后训练中，可以有效地缓解这种安全漏洞，为LLM提供更强的防御能力。

Abstract: Many-shot jailbreaking (MSJ) is an adversarial technique that exploits the long context windows of modern LLMs to circumvent model safety training by including in the prompt many examples of a "fake" assistant responding inappropriately before the final request. With enough examples, the model's in-context learning abilities override its safety training, and it responds as if it were the "fake" assistant. In this work, we probe the effectiveness of different fine-tuning and input sanitization approaches on mitigating MSJ attacks, alone and in combination. We find incremental mitigation effectiveness for each, and show that the combined techniques significantly reduce the effectiveness of MSJ attacks, while retaining model performance in benign in-context learning and conversational tasks.
